# Mobile failure report

**Complete mobile audit.** Generated 2026-10-28T11:30:49.850Z. It attempted all 29 inventoried route paths for each of ten roles at 360px and 390px; four mode/style appearances were measured for each authorized rendered route. Access-denied routes were attempted once per role and were not counted as rendered UI. The sole touch-target exception is an inline anchor within a prose paragraph with surrounding sentence text, as documented in docs/DECISIONS.md.

Total findings: **135328** across 10 distinct causes. Completed collections: **20/20**.
